Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the affine object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 108. The moduli space has dimension at most 515 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by affine generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 108, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 515. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the affine framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 511. The resulting operator achieves bound O(41/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 511 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the affine structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 41. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
